class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self,mid, time, totalTrip)
        trip = 0
        for t in time:
            trip += mid//t
        if trip >= totalTrip:
                return False
        return True

    def minimumTime(self, time: IntList, totalTrips: int) -> int:
        time.sort(reverse=True)
        minimum = min(time)
        left = minimum
        right = minimum*totalTrips
        while left < right:
            mid = (left+ right)//2

            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips:
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1
        return right

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrip: int) -> bool:
        trip = 0
        for t in time:
            trip += mid // t
        return trip >= totalTrip

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        left = min(time)
        right = max(time) * totalTrips
        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1
        return left
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        left = min(time)
        right = min(time) * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
```

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        if totalTrips <= 0:
            return 0

        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        if totalTrips <= 0:
            return 0

        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
```
This is valid.

Option A:
```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return right
```
This returns right. For totalTrips=0, returns 0. For positive, correct. No guard. Is there any issue if totalTrips=0 and time empty? min(time) error. Not concern. ```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return right
```

This is clean. It returns right. If totalTrips=0, returns 0. If totalTrips positive, correct. No guard. But wait, if totalTrips=0, `left = fastest`, `right = 0`, while false, return 0. Good. But if totalTrips=0 and time empty, min error. Not constraints. If we want to handle time empty and totalTrips=0, guard. Not needed.

Should we include guard for totalTrips <=0? If totalTrips=0, return right works. If totalTrips negative, return negative, but not possible. If we include guard, code:
```
if totalTrips <= 0:
    return 0

Potential issue: If the code is run with Python 468.2 and `List`
</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return right
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def satisfiesTrip(self, mid: int, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> bool:
        trips = 0
        for t in time:
            trips += mid // t
        return trips >= totalTrips

    def minimumTime(self, time: List[int], totalTrips: int) -> int:
        fastest = min(time)
        left = fastest
        right = fastest * totalTrips

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            if self.satisfiesTrip(mid, time, totalTrips):
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return right
